Stream Object\",\"\",\"Used as a response for [`defineStreamHandler`](../requests/defineStreamHandler.md)\",\"\",\"**One of the following must be passed** to point to the stream itself\",\"\",\"* ``url`` - string, direct http(s)/ftp(s)/rtmp link to a video stream (protocol support can vary depending on client app capabilities)\",\"* ``ytId`` - string, youtube video ID, plays using the built-in will be selected**\",\"* ``fileIdx`` - number, the index of the video file within the torrent (from `infoHash`), nzb (from `nzbUrl`), rar (from `rarUrls`, zip (from `zipUrls`), 7zip (from `7zipUrls`), tgz (from `tgzUrls`), tar (from `tarUrls`)); (for torrents if fileIdx is not specified, the largest file will be selected)\",\"* ``fileMustInclude`` - string, a string representing a regex (example `\\\"/.mkv$|.mp4$|.avi$|.ts$/i\\\"`) to match the video file within the nzb (from `nzbUrl`), rar (from `rarUrls`, zip (from `zipUrls`), 7zip (from `7zipUrls`), tgz (from `tgzUrls`), tar (from `tarUrls`)); (not supported for torrents yet)\",\"* ``nzbUrl`` - string, http(s) or ftp(s) link to a NZB (usenet) file (this source will also unpack any known archive files), also see: [``Source Limitations``](#source-limitations)\",\"* ``servers`` - array, a list of strings that each represent a connection to a NNTP (usenet) server (for `nzbUrl`) in the form of Objects``](#source-objects) that lead to zip files (multi-volume supported), also see: [``Source Limitations``](#source-limitations)\",\"* ``7zipUrls`` - array, a list of [``Source Objects``](#source-objects) that lead to 7z files (multi-volume supported), also see: [``Source Limitations``](#source-limitations)\",\"* ``tgzUrls`` - array, a list of [``Source Objects``](#source-objects) that lead to tgz files (multi-volume supported), also see: [``Source Limitations``](#source-limitations)\",\"* ``tarUrls`` - array, a list of [``Source Objects``](#source-objects) that lead to tar files (TAR does not support multi-volume), also see: [``Source Limitations``](#source-limitations)\",\"* ``externalUrl`` - string, [``Meta Link``](./meta.links.md) or an external URL to the video, which should be opened in a browser (webpage), e.g. link to Netflix\",\"\",\"### Additional properties to provide information / behaviour flags\",\"\",\"- ``name`` - _optional_ - string, name of the stream; usually used for stream quality\",\"\",\"- ``title`` - _optional_ - string, description of the stream (warning: this will soon be deprecated in favor of `stream.description`)\",\"\",\"- ``description`` - _optional_ - string, description of the stream (previously `stream.title`)\",\"\",\"- ``subtitles`` - _optional_ - array of [``Subtitle objects``](./subtitles.md) representing subtitles for this stream\",\"\",\"- ``sources`` - _optional_ - array of strings, represents a list of torrent tracker URLs and DHT network nodes. This attribute can be used to provide additional peer discovery options when `infoHash` is also specified, but it is not required. If used, each element can be a tracker URL (`tracker:\\u003cprotocol\\u003e://\\u003chost\\u003e:\\u003cport\\u003e`) where `\\u003cprotocol\\u003e` can be either `http` or `udp`. A DHT node (`dht:\\u003cnode_id/info_hash\\u003e`) can also be included.\",\"  \\u003e **WARNING**: Use of DHT may be prohibited by some private trackers as it exposes torrent activity to a broader network, potentially finding more peers.\",\"\",\"- `behaviorHints` (all are optional)\",\"    - `countryWhitelist`: which hints it's restricted to particular countries  - array of ISO 3166-1 alpha-3 country codes **in lowercase** in which the stream is accessible\",\"    - `notWebReady`: applies if the protocol of the url is http(s); needs to be set to `true` if the URL does not support https or is not an MP4 file\",\"    - `bingeGroup`: if defined, addons with the same `behaviorHints.bingeGroup` will be chosen automatically for binge watching; this should be something that identifies the stream's nature within your addon: for example, if your addon is called \\\"gobsAddon\\\", and the stream is 720p, the bingeGroup should be \\\"gobsAddon-720p\\\"; if the next episode has a stream with the same `bingeGroup`, stremio should select that stream implicitly\",\"    - `proxyHeaders`: only applies to `url`s; **When using this property, you must also set `stream.behaviorHints.notWebReady: true`**; This is an object containing `request` and `response` which include the headers that should be used for the stream (example value: `{ \\\"request\\\": { \\\"User-Agent\\\": \\\"Stremio\\\" } }`)\",\"    - `videoHash`: - string, the calculated [OpenSubtitles hash](http://trac.opensubtitles.org/projects/opensubtitles/wiki/HashSourceCodes) of the video, this will be used when the streaming server is not connected (so the hash cannot be calculated locally), this value is passed to subtitle addons to identify correct subtitles\",\"    - `videoSize`: - number, size of the video file in bytes, this value is passed to the subtitle addons to identify correct subtitles\",\"    - `filename`: - string, filename of the video file, although optional, it is highly recommended to set it when using `stream.url` (when possible) in order to identify correct subtitles (addon sdk will show a warning if it is not set in this case), this value is passed to the subtitle addons to identify correct subtitles\",\"\",\"### Source Objects\",\"\",\"An object representing a streaming source, supports the following properties:\",\"\",\"* ``url`` - **required** - string, direct http(s)/ftp(s) link to a file (depending on context: zip, rar, 7z, tar, tgz)\",\"* ``bytes`` - _optional_ - integer, size of the file in bytes - while optional adding this can speed up the initial buffering\",\"\",\"### Source Limitations\",\"\",\"- nzb: will also unpack all known archive types, a nzb files cannot be streamed unless both the first and last segment of the video file are retrievable, PAR2 recovery is not supported\",\"- rar: multi-volume and seeking in the video supported, decompression is not supported (decompression is not normally required for audio / video files)\",\"- zip: multi-volume and decompression are supported, it does not support seeking in the video\",\"- 7zip: multi-volume and LZMA decompression are support, it supports seeking only when compression is not used (decompression is not normally required for audio / video files)\",\"- tar: does not support multi-volume and decompression by design (tar only merges multiple files into one without compressing), seeking is supported\",\"- tgz: multi-volume and decompression are supported, it does not support seeking in the video\"],\"stylingDirectives\":[[[0,16,\"pl-mh\"],[3,16,\"pl-en\"]],[],[[23,24,\"pl-s\"],[24,25,\"pl-s\"],[25,44,\"pl-c1\"],[44,45,\"pl-s\"],[45,46,\"pl-s\"],[46,47,\"pl-s\"],[47,81,\"pl-corl\"],[81,82,\"pl-s\"]],[],[[0,2,\"pl-s\"],[37,39,\"pl-s\"]],[],[[0,1,\"pl-v\"],[2,4,\"pl-s\"],[4,7,\"pl-c1\"],[7,9,\"pl-s\"]],[[0,1,\"pl-v\"],[2,4,\"pl-s\"],[4,8,\"pl-c1\"],[8,10,\"pl-s\"]],[[0,1,\"pl-v\"],[2,4,\"pl-s\"],[4,12,\"pl-c1\"],[12,14,\"pl-s\"],[58,59,\"pl-s\"],[59,66,\"pl-c1\"],[66,67,\"pl-s\"],[119,121,\"pl-s\"],[198,200,\"pl-s\"]],[[0,1,\"pl-v\"],[2,4,\"pl-s\"],[4,11,\"pl-c1\"],[11,13,\"pl-s\"],[77,78,\"pl-s\"],[78,86,\"pl-c1\"],[86,87,\"pl-s\"],[100,101,\"pl-s\"],[101,107,\"pl-c1\"],[107,108,\"pl-s\"],[121,122,\"pl-s\"],[122,129,\"pl-c1\"],[129,130,\"pl-s\"],[142,143,\"pl-s\"],[143,150,\"pl-c1\"],[150,151,\"pl-s\"],[165,166,\"pl-s\"],[166,174,\"pl-c1\"],[174,175,\"pl-s\"],[188,189,\"pl-s\"],[189,196,\"pl-c1\"],[196,197,\"pl-s\"],[210,211,\"pl-s\"],[211,218,\"pl-c1\"],[218,219,\"pl-s\"]],[[0,1,\"pl-v\"],[2,4,\"pl-s\"],[4,19,\"pl-c1\"],[19,21,\"pl-s\"],[71,72,\"pl-s\"],[72,99,\"pl-c1\"],[99,100,\"pl-s\"],[147,148,\"pl-s\"],[148,154,\"pl-c1\"],[154,155,\"pl-s\"],[168,169,\"pl-s\"],[169,176,\"pl-c1\"],[176,177,\"pl-s\"],[189,190,\"pl-s\"],[190,197,\"pl-c1\"],[197,198,\"pl-s\"],[212,213,\"pl-s\"],[213,221,\"pl-c1\"],[221,222,\"pl-s\"],[235,236,\"pl-s\"],[236,243,\"pl-c1\"],[243,244,\"pl-s\"],[257,258,\"pl-s\"],[258,265,\"pl-c1\"],[265,266,\"pl-s\"]],[[0,1,\"pl-v\"],[2,4,\"pl-s\"],[4,10,\"pl-c1\"],[10,12,\"pl-s\"],[135,136,\"pl-s\"],[136,138,\"pl-s\"],[138,156,\"pl-c1\"],[156,158,\"pl-s\"],[158,159,\"pl-s\"],[159,160,\"pl-s\"],[160,179,\"pl-corl\"],[179,180,\"pl-s\"]],[[0,1,\"pl-v\"],[2,4,\"pl-s\"],[4,11,\"pl-c1\"],[11,13,\"pl-s\"],[105,106,\"pl-s\"],[106,112,\"pl-c1\"],[112,113,\"pl-s\"],[130,131,\"pl-s\"],[131,196,\"pl-c1\"],[196,197,\"pl-s\"],[199,200,\"pl-s\"],[200,205,\"pl-c1\"],[205,206,\"pl-s\"],[214,215,\"pl-s\"],[215,219,\"pl-c1\"],[219,220,\"pl-s\"],[248,249,\"p actions</span></div></div></div></div><div></div></div><div class=\"BlobViewContent-module__blobContentWrapper__JS0W6\"><section aria-labelledby=\"file-name-id-wide file-name-id-mobile\" class=\"BlobContent-module__blobContentSection__VOgZq BlobContent-module__blobContentSectionMarkdown__mPLOK\" style=\"margin-top:46px\"><div class=\"js-snippet-clipboard-copy-unpositioned BlobContent-module__markdownBlob__T8jpG\" data-hpc=\"true\" containertiming=\"hpc\"><article class=\"markdown-body entry-content container-lg\" itemprop=\"text\"><div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Stream Object</h2><a id=\"user-content-stream-object\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Stream Object\" href=\"#stream-object\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Used as a response for <a href=\"/Stremio/stremio-addon-sdk/blob/master/docs/api/requests/defineStreamHandler.md\"><code>defineStreamHandler</code></a></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>One of the following must be passed</strong> to point to the stream itself</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><code>url</code> - string, direct http(s)/ftp(s)/rtmp link to a video stream (protocol support can vary depending on client app capabilities)</li>\n<li><code>ytId</code> - string, youtube video ID, plays using the built-in YouTube player</li>\n<li><code>infoHash</code> - string, info hash of a torrent file, and <code>fileIdx</code> is the index of the video file within the torrent; <strong>if fileIdx is not specified, the largest file in the torrent will be selected</strong></li>\n<li><code>fileIdx</code> - number, the index of the video file within the torrent (from <code>infoHash</code>), nzb (from <code>nzbUrl</code>), rar (from <code>rarUrls</code>, zip (from <code>zipUrls</code>), 7zip (from <code>7zipUrls</code>), tgz (from <code>tgzUrls</code>), tar (from <code>tarUrls</code>)); (for torrents if fileIdx is not specified, the largest file will be selected)</li>\n<li><code>fileMustInclude</code> - string, a string representing a regex (example <code>\"/.mkv$|.mp4$|.avi$|.ts$/i\"</code>) to match the video file within the nzb (from <code>nzbUrl</code>), rar (from <code>rarUrls</code>, zip (from <code>zipUrls</code>), 7zip (from <code>7zipUrls</code>), tgz (from <code>tgzUrls</code>), tar (from <code>tarUrls</code>)); (not supported for torrents yet)</li>\n<li><code>nzbUrl</code> - string, http(s) or ftp(s) link to a NZB (usenet) file (this source will also unpack any known archive files), also see: <a href=\"#source-limitations\"><code>Source Limitations</code></a></li>\n<li><code>servers</code> - array, a list of strings that each represent a connection to a NNTP (usenet) server (for <code>nzbUrl</code>) in the form of <code>nntp(s)://{user}:{pass}@{nntpDomain}:{nntpPort}/{nntpConnections}</code> (<code>nntps</code> = SSL; <code>nntp</code> = no encryption) (example: <code>nntps://myuser:mypass@news.example.com:563/4</code>)</li>\n<li><code>rarUrls</code> - array, a list of <a href=\"#source-objects\"><code>Source Objects</code></a> that lead to rar files (multi-volume supported), also see: <a href=\"#source-limitations\"><code>Source Limitations</code></a></li>\n<li><code>zipUrls</code> - array, a list of <a href=\"#source-objects\"><code>Source Objects</code></a> that lead to zip files (multi-volume supported), also see: <a href=\"#source-limitations\"><code>Source Limitations</code></a></li>\n<li><code>7zipUrls</code> - array, a list of <a href=\"#source-objects\"><code>Source Objects</code></a> that lead to 7z files (multi-volume supported), also see: <a href=\"#source-limitations\"><code>Source Limitations</code></a></li>\n<li><code>tgzUrls</code> - array, a list of <a href=\"#source-objects\"><code>Source Objects</code></a> that lead to tgz files (multi-volume supported), also see: <a href=\"#source-limitations\"><code>Source Limitations</code></a></li>\n<li><code>tarUrls</code> - array, a list of <a href=\"#source-objects\"><code>Source Objects</code></a> that lead to tar files (TAR does not support multi-volume), also see: <a href=\"#source-limitations\"><code>Source Limitations</code></a></li>\n<li><code>externalUrl</code> - string, <a href=\"/Stremio/stremio-addon-sdk/blob/master/docs/api/responses/meta.links.md\"><code>Meta Link</code></a> or an external URL to the video, which should be opened in a browser (webpage), e.g. link to Netflix</li>\n</ul>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Additional properties to provide information / behaviour flags</h3><a id=\"user-content-additional-properties-to-provide-information--behaviour-flags\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Additional properties to provide information / behaviour flags\" href=\"#additional-properties-to-provide-information--behaviour-flags\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><code>name</code> - <em>optional</em> - string, name of the stream; usually used for stream quality</p>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><code>title</code> - <em>optional</em> - string, description of the stream (warning: this will soon be deprecated in favor of <code>stream.description</code>)</p>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><code>description</code> - <em>optional</em> - string, description of the stream (previously <code>stream.title</code>)</p>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><code>subtitles</code> - <em>optional</em> - array of <a href=\"/Stremio/stremio-addon-sdk/blob/master/docs/api/responses/subtitles.md\"><code>Subtitle objects</code></a> representing subtitles for this stream</p>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><code>sources</code> - <em>optional</em> - array of strings, represents a list of torrent tracker URLs and DHT network nodes. This attribute can be used to provide additional peer discovery options when <code>infoHash</code> is also specified, but it is not required. If used, each element can be a tracker URL (<code>tracker:&lt;protocol&gt;://&lt;host&gt;:&lt;port&gt;</code>) where <code>&lt;protocol&gt;</code> can be either <code>http</code> or <code>udp</code>. A DHT node (<code>dht:&lt;node_id/info_hash&gt;</code>) can also be included.</p>\n<blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>WARNING</strong>: Use of DHT may be prohibited by some private trackers as it exposes torrent activity to a broader network, potentially finding more peers.</p>\n</blockquote>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><code>behaviorHints</code> (all are optional)</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><code>countryWhitelist</code>: which hints it's restricted to particular countries  - array of ISO 3166-1 alpha-3 country codes <strong>in lowercase</strong> in which the stream is accessible</li>\n<li><code>notWebReady</code>: applies if the protocol of the url is http(s); needs to be set to <code>true</code> if the URL does not support https or is not an MP4 file</li>\n<li><code>bingeGroup</code>: if defined, addons with the same <code>behaviorHints.bingeGroup</code> will be chosen automatically for binge watching; this should be something that identifies the stream's nature within your addon: for example, if your addon is called \"gobsAddon\", and the stream is 720p, the bingeGroup should be \"gobsAddon-720p\"; if the next episode has a stream with the same <code>bingeGroup</code>, stremio should select that stream implicitly</li>\n<li><code>proxyHeaders</code>: only applies to <code>url</code>s; <strong>When using this property, you must also set <code>stream.behaviorHints.notWebReady: true</code></strong>; This is an object containing <code>request</code> and <code>response</code> which include the headers that should be used for the stream (example value: <code>{ \"request\": { \"User-Agent\": \"Stremio\" } }</code>)</li>\n<li><code>videoHash</code>: - string, the calculated <a href=\"http://trac.opensubtitles.org/projects/opensubtitles/wiki/HashSourceCodes\" rel=\"nofollow\">OpenSubtitles hash</a> of the video, this will be used when the streaming server is not connected (so the hash cannot be calculated locally), this value is passed to subtitle addons to identify correct subtitles</li>\n<li><code>videoSize</code>: - number, size of the video file in bytes, this value is passed to the subtitle addons to identify correct subtitles</li>\n<li><code>filename</code>: - string, filename of the video file, although optional, it is highly recommended to set it when using <code>stream.url</code> (when possible) in order to identify correct subtitles (addon sdk will show a warning if it is not set in this case), this value is passed to the subtitle addons to identify correct subtitles</li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n</ul>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Source Objects</h3><a id=\"user-content-source-objects\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Source Objects\" href=\"#source-objects\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">An object representing a streaming source, supports the following properties:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><code>url</code> - <strong>required</strong> - string, direct http(s)/ftp(s) link to a file (depending on context: zip, rar, 7z, tar, tgz)</li>\n<li><code>bytes</code> - <em>optional</em> - integer, size of the file in bytes - while optional adding this can speed up the initial buffering</li>\n</ul>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Source Limitations</h3><a id=\"user-content-source-limitations\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Source Limitations\" href=\"#source-limitations\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>nzb: will also unpack all known archive types, a nzb files cannot be streamed unless both the first and last segment of the video file are retrievable, PAR2 recovery is not supported</li>\n<li>rar: multi-volume and seeking in the video supported, decompression is not supported (decompression is not normally required for audio / video files)</li>\n<li>zip: multi-volume and decompression are supported, it does not support seeking in the video</li>\n<li>7zip: multi-volume and LZMA decompression are support, it supports seeking only when compression is not used (decompression is not normally required for audio / video files)</li>\n<li>tar: does not support multi-volume and decompression by design (tar only merges multiple files into one without compressing), seeking is supported</li>\n<li>tgz: multi-volume and decompression are supported, it does not support seeking in the video</li>\n</ul>\n</article></div><div class=\"d-none\"></div></section></div></div></div> </div> <!-- --> </div></div></div></div></div></div><div
